- **memobank**: Recalls and persists coding knowledge across sessions using the memo CLI. Writes memories when a non-obvious bug is fixed, an architecture decision is made, a repeatable process is discovered, or codebase structure is mapped. Distills and promotes memories across tiers over time. Use when: the user's first message involves code or files, after fixing a non-obvious bug, after making a tech or design decision, before switching project context, or when a recalled memory turns out to be wrong. NOT for: projects without a .memobank/ directory or memo CLI installed. ([clawde-agent/memobank-cli](https://github.com/clawde-agent/memobank-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/memobank) · ★2)

- **memoir**: Draft, review, revise, and audit chaptered narrative nonfiction (family memoirs, oral histories, biography-from-archive, interview-grounded long-form profiles) reconstructed from a private evidentiary corpus. - **memoir**: Memory lifecycle management for Claude Code agents. memoir handles what built-in memory doesn't: hot/cold layering, weight evolution, decay, archiving, merging, and autonomous self-maintenance across sessions. Use when the user wants to set up or maintain a memory system, asks about "memoir", mentions memory lifecycle/organization, says "/memoir", or when a session is ending and memory maintenance is due.
